Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The monte_carlo operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 22. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 841 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 22 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the monte_carlo structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 841. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
